## Appendix B Summary of Library Facilities

This appendix is a complete list of the facilities declared within the header files supplied with the GNU C Library. Each entry also lists the standard or other source from which each facility is derived, and tells you where in the manual you can find more information about how to use it.

`ACCOUNTING`  
`utmp.h` (SVID): [Manipulating the User Accounting Database](https://sourceware.org/glibc/manual/latest/html_node/Manipulating-the-Database.html).

`AF_FILE`  
`sys/socket.h` (GNU): [Address Formats](https://sourceware.org/glibc/manual/latest/html_node/Address-Formats.html).

`AF_INET`  
`sys/socket.h` (BSD): [Address Formats](https://sourceware.org/glibc/manual/latest/html_node/Address-Formats.html).

`AF_INET6`  
`sys/socket.h` (IPv6 Basic API): [Address Formats](https://sourceware.org/glibc/manual/latest/html_node/Address-Formats.html).

`AF_LOCAL`  
`sys/socket.h` (POSIX): [Address Formats](https://sourceware.org/glibc/manual/latest/html_node/Address-Formats.html).

`AF_UNIX`  
`sys/socket.h` (BSD): [Address Formats](https://sourceware.org/glibc/manual/latest/html_node/Address-Formats.html).

`sys/socket.h` (Unix98): [Address Formats](https://sourceware.org/glibc/manual/latest/html_node/Address-Formats.html).

`AF_UNSPEC`  
`sys/socket.h` (BSD): [Address Formats](https://sourceware.org/glibc/manual/latest/html_node/Address-Formats.html).

`tcflag_t ALTWERASE`  
`termios.h` (BSD): [Local Modes](https://sourceware.org/glibc/manual/latest/html_node/Local-Modes.html).
